## 拼写说明

英式 / 美式拼写均应结合题目和录音判断，不要把等号两边当成两种不同含义。

- centre = center
- socialise = socialize
- licence = license
- archaeology = archeology
- maths = math
- fertiliser = fertilizer
- mediaeval = medieval
- adviser = advisor
- verbalisation = verbalization
- enquiry = inquiry
- organisation = organization
- catalogue = catalog
- colour = color
- jewellery = jewelry
- odour = odor
- flavour = flavor
- rumour = rumor
- fibre = fiber
- tyre = tire
- specialised = specialized
- manoeuvre = maneuver
- recognisable = recognizable
- tranquiliser = tranquilizer
- appetising = appetizing
- artefact = artifact
- archaeologist = archeologist
- urbanisation = urbanization
- armour = armor
- mechanisation = mechanization

上表按常见拼写规范整理；原 PDF 个别拼写可能含排版问题，未据此改写后文词条。
